# Table

Category: Data display. Data table with quiet hairlines, muted sentence-case headers and hover rows, styled on plain table elements under .ayy-table. Optional frame with toolbar and pager; densities, column lines, sticky header, pinned and resizable columns; sortable, selectable (including drag-to-select across rows), expandable, grouped, linked and disabled rows; column menus with icons, insertion and wrapping; inline editable, empty, truncated and media cells; row actions. Cell content stays inside its column and ends in … unless the column wraps. Also called: table, data-table, datagrid.

**Classes**
- `.ayy-table-container` — Optional bordered frame on the page colour: .ayy-table__toolbar, the .ayy-table-wrap, a .ayy-table__pager. With a max-block-size its table scrolls inside.
- `.ayy-table__toolbar` — Bar above the table: a __heading and __tools.
- `.ayy-table__heading` — Stack of __title and __description.
- `.ayy-table__title` — The table's <h2>.
- `.ayy-table__description` — Muted line under the title.
- `.ayy-table__tools` — Toolbar's end cluster: search, filters, a columns menu, a button.
- `.ayy-table__pager` — Bar under the table: the range shown and a Pagination.
- `.ayy-table-wrap` — Scroll container around the table.
- `.ayy-table` — On the <table>. Styles thead/tbody/tfoot/tr/th/td/caption inside.
- `.ayy-table--compact` — Tighter rows (2rem headers).
- `.ayy-table--spacious` — Roomier rows and columns.
- `.ayy-table--bordered` — Lines between the columns too.
- `.ayy-table--sticky` — Header and footer rows stick while the wrap or container scrolls.
- `.ayy-table--fixed` — table-layout: fixed.
- `.ayy-table--reorderable` — Draggable header cells that reorder columns on drop.
- `.ayy-table__draggable` — On a th: indicates this column header can be dragged to reorder columns.
- `.ayy-table__column-header` — Flex row holding the column label, optional icon and column menu.
- `.ayy-table__column-label` — Column label or sort button; takes the room the menu leaves and ends in … when the column is narrower.
- `.ayy-table__column-icon` — Optional icon before a column's label.
- `.ayy-table__add-column` — Narrow trailing column with an accessible add-column button.
- `.ayy-table__wrap` — On th/td: content wraps within the column instead of staying on one line and ending in ….
- `.ayy-table__num` — On th/td with numbers: end-aligned, tabular digits.
- `.ayy-table__sort` — A <button> inside a sortable <th> (or, with aria-haspopup="menu", the title that opens the column menu), its label in a __column-label, the whole header cell clickable, an arrow after the text only once sorted. aria-sort on the <th> marks the sorted column.
- `.ayy-table__resize` — Column resize separator (<span tabindex="0">) at a header cell's end: drag, arrow keys; double-click resets.
- `.ayy-table__select` — On the th/td holding a row's checkbox (and the select-all box): as narrow as the box.
- `.ayy-table__reorder` — On the th/td holding a row's .ayy-drag-handle: as narrow as the handle. The row drags by it; a primary line shows where it lands.
- `.ayy-table__pin` — On a column's th/td: sticks to the inline start when scrolling sideways.
- `.ayy-table__pin--end` — With __pin: sticks to the inline end.
- `.ayy-table__truncate` — On a <span> in a cell: one line ending in …, at most --ayy-table-truncate (16rem).
- `.ayy-table__edit` — On an Inline edit (.ayy-inline-edit) in a cell: editable text that reads like cell text at rest, outlines on hover, turns into a field in place with the text where it was, saves on Enter or blur and reverts on Escape.
- `.ayy-table__media` — Flex row in a cell: an Avatar or icon, then text.
- `.ayy-table__secondary` — Muted second line in a cell.
- `.ayy-table__expand` — Chevron <button aria-expanded> that shows the __detail row after its row.
- `.ayy-table__detail` — The <tr> after an expandable row; hidden while its __expand is aria-expanded="false".
- `.ayy-table__group` — A <tr> heading a group: one <th colspan scope="rowgroup"> with its label in a <span>. One per <tbody>.
- `.ayy-table__empty` — On the <td colspan> of a body's only row when nothing matches.
- `.ayy-table__row-link` — The row's main <a>; its ::after covers the row. Other controls stay above it.
- `.ayy-table__actions` — On the trailing th/td holding row actions: end-aligned, narrow.
- `.ayy-table__row-actions` — Cluster of row action buttons and the ⋯ menu trigger.
- `.ayy-table__row-actions--reveal` — Shows only on row hover, focus, selection or open menu (always on touch).

**States**
- `default` — Collapsed table, s text; header row on a wash fill with muted sentence-case labels; hairlines between rows.
- `hover` (`tbody tr:hover; the sort, expand, resize and edit controls; a row link`) — Row gets a wash fill (not group, detail, empty or disabled rows) and reveals __row-actions--reveal; a sortable or draggable header shows a pointer cursor and gets a wash fill; controls brighten, .ayy-table__edit gets a subtle line outline; a row link underlines.
- `pressed` — doesn't apply: A sort or drag header keeps its pointer cursor; use the dragging state for a header being moved.
- `focus` (`:focus-visible on the sort button, expand button, resize handle or row link; :focus-visible on the .ayy-table__edit field`) — 2px ring (an editing field gets a ring border and surface fill); a row link rings the whole row.
- `disabled` (`tr[aria-disabled="true"]`) — Muted text, no hover fill. Forced colours: GrayText.
- `selected` (`tr[aria-selected="true"] (set by ticking a checkbox, Shift+click, or dragging and holding the cursor across rows)`) — Wash fill (under pinned cells too) and a 2px primary bar on the start edge. Forced colours: Highlight.
- `error` (`.ayy-table__edit[aria-invalid="true"]`) — Destructive border on an invalid inline edit field.
- `loading` (`aria-busy="true" on the <table>`) — Body rows fade to 50%; first loads use Skeleton rows.
- `sorted` (`th[aria-sort]`) — Label turns text colour; only the arrow that applies shows.
- `expanded` (`.ayy-table__expand[aria-expanded="true"]`) — Chevron points up; the detail row shows.
- `resizing` (`.ayy-table__resize[data-dragging]`) — 2px ring-colour line; col-resize cursor.
- `reordering` (`th[data-dragging] or th[data-drop-edge]`) — Dragged header dims to 40%; target header shows a 2px primary drop line and top pin marker on the landing edge.
- `row-reordering` (`tr[data-dragging] or tr[data-drop-edge]`) — Dragged row dims to 40%; the neighbour it will land beside gets a 2px primary line on that edge. Forced colours: Highlight border.

**Sizes**
- Density — Headers 2.25rem, cells padded 12px; compact 2rem headers, tighter rows; spacious 2.75rem, 16px. Text follows data-density.
- Width — Fills its wrap, which scrolls when columns don't fit. After a column resize it stays at least as wide as its wrap, even with no data columns.

**Custom element** `<ayy-table>` (@danitesler/ayywi/elements) — 
- event `ayy-sort`: { column, key, direction } before the rows move (key: data-sort-key). preventDefault() to sort them yourself.
- event `ayy-selection-change`: { rows } — the selected <tr>s.
- event `ayy-expand-change`: { row, expanded }
- event `ayy-column-resize`: { column, key, width } when a resize ends (width null after a reset).
- event `ayy-cell-edit`: { row, cell, column, key, value } when an inline edit in a cell saves a change.
- event `ayy-column-reorder`: { from, to, key, targetKey } when a column is dragged to a new position.
- event `ayy-row-reorder`: { from, to, id } when a row is dropped, or moved with the arrow keys on its handle. from and to count rows with a handle; to is what Array.splice wants after removing the row. id is the row's data-id.
- event `ayy-column-action`: { action, column, key, value } before a column action. Cancel to handle it in application state. data-column-action: sort-ascending, sort-descending, duplicate, hide, delete, freeze, wrap, icon (data-column-value), insert-before/insert-after (logical order), insert-left/insert-right (physical direction), add, filter, group, group-by (data-column-value). Filter/group open the toolbar button with data-table-control=filters/groups.

**React** — `import { Table, TableContainer, TableToolbar, TableHeading, TableTitle, TableDescription, TableTools, TablePager, TableHeader, TableBody, TableFooter, TableRow, TableHead, TableCell, TableEdit, TableResize, TableExpand, TableGroup, TableEmpty, TableRowLink, TableMedia, TableSecondary, TableRowActions, TableCaption } from "@danitesler/ayywi/react";`
- `<Table>` renders <div class="ayy-table-wrap"><table>. Props: `compact` boolean; `spacious` boolean; `bordered` boolean; `sticky` boolean; `fixed` boolean; `reorderable` boolean; `onColumnResize` ({ column, key, width }) => void; `onCellEdit` ({ row, cell, column, key, value }) => void; `onColumnReorder` ({ from, to, key, targetKey }) => boolean | void; `onRowReorder` ({ from, to, id }) => boolean | void — a row's DragHandle was dropped or moved by key. React doesn't move rows itself: reorder your data and return false.; `onColumnAction` ({ action, column, key, value }) => boolean | void — return false to update application state; `wrapperProps` props for the .ayy-table-wrap (a max-block-size for sticky)
- `<TableContainer>` renders <div class="ayy-table-container">.
- `<TableToolbar>` renders <div class="ayy-table__toolbar">.
- `<TableHeading>` renders <div class="ayy-table__heading">.
- `<TableTitle>` renders <h2 class="ayy-table__title">.
- `<TableDescription>` renders <p class="ayy-table__description">.
- `<TableTools>` renders <div class="ayy-table__tools">.
- `<TablePager>` renders <div class="ayy-table__pager">.
- `<TableHeader>` renders <thead>.
- `<TableBody>` renders <tbody>.
- `<TableFooter>` renders <tfoot>.
- `<TableRow>` renders <tr>. Props: `selected` boolean; `disabled` boolean — aria-disabled; `detail` boolean — the row under an expandable row
- `<TableHead>` renders <th scope="col">; a sort <button> when sortable; a TableResize when resizable. Props: `numeric` boolean; `select` boolean; `actions` boolean; `pin` boolean | "start" | "end"; `sort` "ascending" | "descending" | "none"; `onSort` () => void — flip with nextSortDirection(); `resizable` boolean; `reorderable` boolean; `icon` ReactNode — before the label; `menu` ReactNode — a composed DropdownMenu beside the label; `wrap` boolean — wraps the header label instead of ending it in …
- `<TableCell>` renders <td>. Props: `numeric` boolean; `select` boolean; `reorder` boolean — the narrow column holding a row's DragHandle; `actions` boolean; `pin` boolean | "start" | "end"; `truncate` boolean — wraps content in a .ayy-table__truncate span; `wrap` boolean — wraps long cell content instead of ending it in …
- `<TableEdit>` renders <InlineEdit className="ayy-table__edit"> — a display button and an <input>, or a <textarea> when multiline. Props: `value` string — controlled cell text; `defaultValue` string — uncontrolled starting text; `aria-label` string — names the button and the field: "Plan for Northwind"; `displayValue` ReactNode — what the cell shows at rest when it isn't the value (a formatted price); `multiline` boolean — textarea; Shift+Enter adds a line, ⌘Enter or Ctrl+Enter saves; `disabled` boolean; `placeholder` string — shown when the cell is empty; `className` string — extra classes on the root; `inputMode` string — the input's virtual keyboard; `onValueCommit` (value: string) => void — called on Enter or blur when the value changed
- `<TableResize>` renders <span class="ayy-table__resize" role="separator" aria-orientation="vertical" tabindex="0">.
- `<TableExpand>` renders <button type="button" class="ayy-table__expand" aria-expanded>. Props: `expanded` boolean
- `<TableGroup>` renders <tr class="ayy-table__group"><th colspan scope="rowgroup"><span>. Props: `colSpan` number
- `<TableEmpty>` renders <tr><td colspan class="ayy-table__empty">. Props: `colSpan` number
- `<TableRowLink>` renders <a class="ayy-table__row-link">.
- `<TableMedia>` renders <div class="ayy-table__media">.
- `<TableSecondary>` renders <span class="ayy-table__secondary">.
- `<TableRowActions>` renders <div class="ayy-table__row-actions">. Props: `reveal` boolean
- `<TableCaption>` renders <caption>.

**Accessibility**
- Use real <th> cells with scope (React sets scope="col"); a cell naming its row can be <th scope="row">.
- Name the table: a <caption>, or aria-labelledby pointing at the .ayy-table__title.
- Sort buttons are reachable by Tab and announced with aria-sort; only the sorted column carries it.
- Row checkboxes, expand buttons and row action buttons need aria-labels naming their row ("Select INV-1042", "Show details for INV-1042"); point an expand button's aria-controls at its detail row.
- Resize handles are focusable separators with aria-valuenow in px: arrow keys resize (Shift: bigger steps), Home/End jump to the limits.
- Set aria-busy="true" while rows load, and aria-disabled="true" on rows that can't be acted on (disable their controls too).

**Do**
- Use a table to compare items across the same attributes.
- End-align numbers with numeric; a cell keeps to one line, so put the full text in its title and add wrap to a column that must show it all.
- Make scanned columns sortable; put the raw value in data-sort-value when the cell shows a formatted one.
- Frame a working table with TableContainer: title, search and a columns menu above, Pagination below.
- Compose a column menu with TableHead menu and icon, or make the title itself the trigger (a .ayy-table__sort button with aria-haspopup="menu" as the TableHead child, set aria-sort on the th): clicking the title opens the menu, which holds a Sort submenu, Duplicate, Hide and Delete, and dragging it still reorders. data-column-action wires sort-ascending/sort-descending (a Sort submenu), duplicate, hide, delete, freeze, wrap, icon, insert-before/after, add and shared filter/group controls. Return false from onColumnAction or cancel ayy-column-action to own the data changes.
- Pin the name column on wide tables; use sticky with a max-block-size on long ones.
- Use expandable rows for details that don't deserve a column, and group rows to split a list by status or date.
- Put quick actions and a ⋯ DropdownMenu in a trailing actions cell; pair selection with an ActionBar for bulk actions.
- Show TableEmpty with an EmptyState when a search matches nothing.

**Don't**
- Don't use tables for layout, or for rows with one attribute — use a List.
- Don't zebra-stripe; hairlines and hover are enough.
- Don't nest controls in every cell — one trailing actions column is plenty.
- Don't put a row link in a pinned cell: its cover stays inside the sticky cell.
- Don't resize columns under a header with more than one row.

## Table — Simple

HTML (also Vue/Svelte/Angular templates, server templates):

```html
<!-- Rendered from simple.tsx. -->
<div class="ayy-table-wrap"><table class="ayy-table"><caption class="ayy-sr-only">Accounts</caption><thead><tr><th scope="col">Account</th><th scope="col">Plan</th><th scope="col">Status</th><th scope="col" class="ayy-table__num">Seats</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Northwind Traders</td><td>Enterprise</td><td>Active</td><td class="ayy-table__num">240</td></tr><tr><td>Lumen Labs</td><td>Team</td><td>Past due</td><td class="ayy-table__num">32</td></tr><tr><td>Acme Studio</td><td>Team</td><td>Trial</td><td class="ayy-table__num">12</td></tr><tr><td>Brightside Health</td><td>Enterprise</td><td>Active</td><td class="ayy-table__num">610</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
```

React:

```tsx
import { Table, TableBody, TableCaption, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@danitesler/ayywi/react";

const accounts = [
  { name: "Northwind Traders", plan: "Enterprise", status: "Active", seats: 240 },
  { name: "Lumen Labs", plan: "Team", status: "Past due", seats: 32 },
  { name: "Acme Studio", plan: "Team", status: "Trial", seats: 12 },
  { name: "Brightside Health", plan: "Enterprise", status: "Active", seats: 610 },
];

export default function Example() {
  return (
    <Table>
      <TableCaption className="ayy-sr-only">Accounts</TableCaption>
      <TableHeader>
        <TableRow>
          <TableHead>Account</TableHead>
          <TableHead>Plan</TableHead>
          <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
          <TableHead numeric>Seats</TableHead>
        </TableRow>
      </TableHeader>
      <TableBody>
        {accounts.map((account) => (
          <TableRow key={account.name}>
            <TableCell>{account.name}</TableCell>
            <TableCell>{account.plan}</TableCell>
            <TableCell>{account.status}</TableCell>
            <TableCell numeric>{account.seats}</TableCell>
          </TableRow>
        ))}
      </TableBody>
    </Table>
  );
}
```

class="ayy-table__num">48</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></ayy-table>

React:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Badge, Checkbox, Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableContainer, TableDescription,
  TableHead, TableHeader, TableHeading, TableRow, TableTitle, TableToolbar,
  compareValues, nextSortDirection, type SortDirection,
} from "@danitesler/ayywi/react";

const accounts = [
  { name: "Northwind Traders", plan: "Enterprise", status: "Active", seats: 240 },
  { name: "Lumen Labs", plan: "Team", status: "Past due", seats: 32 },
  { name: "Acme Studio", plan: "Team", status: "Trial", seats: 12 },
  { name: "Brightside Health", plan: "Enterprise", status: "Active", seats: 610 },
  { name: "Kestrel Logistics", plan: "Starter", status: "Closed", seats: 4 },
  { name: "Orchard & Pine", plan: "Team", status: "Active", seats: 48 },
] as const;
const statusBadge = { Active: "success", Trial: "info", "Past due": "destructive", Closed: "muted" } as const;

export default function Example() {
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<{ key: "name" | "seats"; direction: SortDirection } | null>(null);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const rows = sort ? [...accounts].sort((a, b) => compareValues(a[sort.key], b[sort.key], sort.direction)) : accounts;
  const sortBy = (key: "name" | "seats") => {
    setSort((current) => ({ key, direction: nextSortDirection(current?.key === key ? current.direction : null) }));
  };

  return (
    <TableContainer style={{ inlineSize: "100%" }}>
      <TableToolbar>
        <TableHeading>
          <TableTitle id="tm-title">Accounts</TableTitle>
          <TableDescription>Sort by account or seats, and select rows.</TableDescription>
        </TableHeading>
      </TableToolbar>
      <Table aria-labelledby="tm-title">
        <TableHeader>
          <TableRow>
            <TableHead select>
              <Checkbox
                aria-label="Select all accounts"
                checked={selected.length === accounts.length}
                indeterminate={selected.length > 0 && selected.length < accounts.length}
                onCheckedChange={(checked) => setSelected(checked ? accounts.map((account) => account.name) : [])}
              />
            </TableHead>
            <TableHead sort={sort?.key === "name" ? sort.direction : "none"} onSort={() => sortBy("name")}>Account</TableHead>
            <TableHead>Plan</TableHead>
            <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
            <TableHead numeric sort={sort?.key === "seats" ? sort.direction : "none"} onSort={() => sortBy("seats")}>Seats</TableHead>
          </TableRow>
        </TableHeader>
        <TableBody>
          {rows.map((account) => (
            <TableRow key={account.name} selected={selected.includes(account.name)}>
              <TableCell select>
                <Checkbox
                  aria-label={`Select ${account.name}`}
                  checked={selected.includes(account.name)}
                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => setSelected((current) => checked ? [...current, account.name] : current.filter((name) => name !== account.name))}
                />
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell>{account.name}</TableCell>
              <TableCell>{account.plan}</TableCell>
              <TableCell><Badge variant={statusBadge[account.status]}>{account.status}</Badge></TableCell>
              <TableCell numeric>{account.seats}</TableCell>
            </TableRow>
          ))}
        </TableBody>
      </Table>
    </TableContainer>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// A picked icon's value is in COLUMN_ICONS; anything else is an emoji, shown as text.
const columnIcon = (value: string) => {
  const found = COLUMN_ICONS.find((item) => item.value === value);
  return found ? <Icon icon={found.icon as typeof StarIcon} name={found.name} /> : value;
};
interface Column { key: ColumnKey; label: string; numeric?: boolean; icon?: string }
const COLUMNS: Column[] = [
  { key: "name", label: "Account" },
  { key: "plan", label: "Plan" },
  { key: "status", label: "Status" },
  { key: "seats", label: "Seats", numeric: true },
  { key: "mrr", label: "MRR", numeric: true },
  { key: "renews", label: "Renews" },
  { key: "note", label: "Last note" },
];

const money = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: "USD", maximumFractionDigits: 0 });
const day = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { month: "short", day: "numeric", year: "numeric" });
const PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS = [5, 10, 25];

type Density = "compact" | "default" | "spacious";

/**
 * One table, every variation: open Props to switch the density, column lines, the frame (toolbar and pager), a sticky
 * header, pinned columns, resizable columns, inline cell editing, selectable (including drag-to-select across rows),
 * expandable, grouped, linked and disabled rows, truncated text and the loading look. Search for a name that isn't
 * there to see the empty row.
 */
export default function Example({ setControls }: { setControls?: (node: ReactNode) => void } = {}) {
  const [density, setDensity] = useState<Density>("default");
  const [frame, setFrame] = useState(true);
  const [bordered, setBordered] = useState(false);
  const [sticky, setSticky] = useState(true);
  const [pinName, setPinName] = useState(true);
  const [pinActions, setPinActions] = useState(false);
  const [resizable, setResizable] = useState(true);
  const [reorderable, setReorderable] = useState(true);
  const [rowReorder, setRowReorder] = useState(true);
  const [editable, setEditable] = useState(true);
  const [selectable, setSelectable] = useState(true);
  const [expandable, setExpandable] = useState(true);
  const [grouped, setGrouped] = useState(false);
  const [rowLinks, setRowLinks] = useState(false);
  const [disabledRows, setDisabledRows] = useState(true);
  const [reveal, setReveal] = useState(true);
  const [truncate, setTruncate] = useState(true);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<Account[]>(ACCOUNTS);
  const [columns, setColumns] = useState(COLUMNS);
  const [columnOrder, setColumnOrder] = useState<ColumnKey[]>(COLUMNS.map((c) => c.key));
  const [frozen, setFrozen] = useState<ColumnKey[]>([]);
  const [wrapped, setWrapped] = useState<ColumnKey[]>([]);
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, Record<string, string>>>({});
  const [groupKey, setGroupKey] = useState<ColumnKey>("status");
  const filterTrigger = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const groupTrigger = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const nextColumn = useRef(0);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<Status[]>([]);
  const [planFilter, setPlanFilter] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [hidden, setHidden] = useState<ColumnKey[]>([]);
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<{ key: ColumnKey; direction: SortDirection } | null>({ key: "mrr", direction: "descending" });
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string[]>(["ACC-206"]);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<string[]>(["ACC-201"]);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(10);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!setControls) return;
    const toggle = (id: string, label: string, checked: boolean, set: (v: boolean) => void, disabled = false) => (
      <div className="ayy-spread">
        <Label htmlFor={id}>{label}</Label>
        <Switch id={id} checked={checked} onCheckedChange={set} disabled={disabled} />
      </div>
    );
    setControls(
      <div className="ayy-stack">
        <div className="ayy-spread">
          <Label htmlFor="tp-density">Density</Label>
          <div style={{ inlineSize: "7.5rem" }}>
            <Select id="tp-density" size="sm" value={density} onChange={(e) => setDensity(e.target.value as Density)}>
              <option value="compact">Compact</option>
              <option value="default">Default</option>
              <option value="spacious">Spacious</option>
            </Select>
          </div>
        </div>
        <span className="ayy-muted ayy-mono ayy-small">Table</span>
        {toggle("tp-frame", "Frame, toolbar and pager", frame, setFrame)}
        {toggle("tp-bordered", "Column lines", bordered, setBordered)}
        {toggle("tp-sticky", "Sticky header", sticky, setSticky)}
        {toggle("tp-pin", "Pin the name column", pinName, setPinName)}
        {toggle("tp-pin-end", "Pin the actions column", pinActions, setPinActions)}
        {toggle("tp-resize", "Resizable columns", resizable, setResizable)}
        {toggle("tp-reorder", "Reorderable columns", reorderable, setReorderable)}
        {toggle("tp-row-reorder", "Reorderable rows", rowReorder, setRowReorder)}
        {toggle("tp-edit", "Editable cells", editable, setEditable)}
        {toggle("tp-truncate", "Truncate long text", truncate, setTruncate)}
        {toggle("tp-loading", "Loading", loading, setLoading)}
        <span className="ayy-muted ayy-mono ayy-small">Rows</span>
        {toggle("tp-select", "Selectable", selectable, setSelectable)}
        {toggle("tp-expand", "Expandable", expandable, setExpandable)}
        {toggle("tp-group", "Grouped by status", grouped, (on) => { setGroupKey("status"); setGrouped(on); })}
        {toggle("tp-link", "Whole row is a link", rowLinks, setRowLinks, pinName)}
        {toggle("tp-disabled", "Closed accounts disabled", disabledRows, setDisabledRows)}
        {toggle("tp-reveal", "Actions on hover", reveal, setReveal)}
      </div>,
    );
  }, [setControls, density, frame, bordered, sticky, pinName, pinActions, resizable, reorderable, rowReorder, editable, selectable, expandable, grouped, rowLinks, disabledRows, reveal, truncate, loading]);

  useEffect(() => () => setControls?.(null), [setControls]);

  const updateAccount = (id: string, patch: Partial<Account>) =>
    setAccounts((list) => list.map((a) => (a.id === id ? { ...a, ...patch } : a)));

  const visibleKeys = columnOrder.filter((k) => !hidden.includes(k));
  const visible = visibleKeys.map((k) => columns.find((c) => c.key === k)!);
  const isPinned = (key: ColumnKey) => key === "name" ? pinName : frozen.includes(key);
  const columnProps = (key: ColumnKey) => ({ "data-key": key, pin: isPinned(key), wrap: wrapped.includes(key) });
  const valueOf = (account: Account, key: ColumnKey) => key in account ? account[key as Key] : values[account.id]?.[key] ?? "";
  const shownValue = (account: Account, key: ColumnKey) => {
    const value = valueOf(account, key);
    return key === "mrr" ? money.format(account.mrr) : key === "renews" && account.renews !== "—" ? day.format(new Date(`${account.renews}T00:00:00`)) : String(value ?? "");
  };
  const insertColumn = (key?: ColumnKey, after = false) => {
    const column: Column = { key: `column-${++nextColumn.current}`, label: `Column ${nextColumn.current}` };
    setColumns((list) => [...list, column]);
    setColumnOrder((list) => {
      const next = [...list];
      const index = key === undefined ? next.length : next.indexOf(key) + (after ? 1 : 0);
      next.splice(index, 0, column.key);
      return next;
    });
  };
  const onColumnAction = ({ action, key, value }: TableColumnActionDetail) => {
    const columnKey = key as ColumnKey | undefined;
    if (action === "add") insertColumn();
    else if ((action === "sort-ascending" || action === "sort-descending") && columnKey) setSort({ key: columnKey, direction: action === "sort-ascending" ? "ascending" : "descending" });
    else if (action === "duplicate" && columnKey) {
      const source = columns.find((c) => c.key === columnKey)!;
      const copy: Column = { ...source, key: `column-${++nextColumn.current}`, label: `${source.label} copy` };
      setColumns((list) => [...list, copy]);
      setColumnOrder((list) => {
        const next = [...list];
        next.splice(next.indexOf(columnKey) + 1, 0, copy.key);
        return next;
      });
      setValues((all) => Object.fromEntries(accounts.map((a) => [a.id, { ...all[a.id], [copy.key]: shownValue(a, columnKey) }])));
    } else if (action === "hide" && columnKey) {
      setHidden((list) => [...list, columnKey]);
    } else if (action === "delete" && columnKey) {
      setColumns((list) => list.filter((c) => c.key !== columnKey));
      setColumnOrder((list) => list.filter((k) => k !== columnKey));
      setSort((current) => (current?.key === columnKey ? null : current));
    } else if (action === "insert-before" || action === "insert-after") insertColumn(columnKey, action === "insert-after");
    else if (action === "freeze" && columnKey) {
      if (columnKey === "name") setPinName((on) => !on);
      else setFrozen((list) => list.includes(columnKey) ? list.filter((k) => k !== columnKey) : [...list, columnKey]);
    } else if (action === "wrap" && columnKey) setWrapped((list) => list.includes(columnKey) ? list.filter((k) => k !== columnKey) : [...list, columnKey]);
    else if (action === "icon" && columnKey) setColumns((list) => list.map((c) => c.key === columnKey ? { ...c, icon: value || undefined } : c));
    else if (action === "filter" || action === "group") setTimeout(() => (action === "filter" ? filterTrigger : groupTrigger).current?.click(), 0);
    else if (action === "group-by") {
      setGrouped(value !== "none");
      if (value && value !== "none") setGroupKey(value as ColumnKey);
    }
    return false;
  };
  const columnMenu = (column: Column) => (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
        <button type="button" className="ayy-table__sort">
          <span className="ayy-table__column-label">
            {column.icon ? <span className="ayy-table__column-icon" aria-hidden="true">{columnIcon(column.icon)}</span> : null}
            {column.label}
          </span>
        </button>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
      <DropdownMenuContent>
        <DropdownMenuLabel>{column.label}</DropdownMenuLabel>
        <DropdownMenuSub>
          <DropdownMenuSubTrigger icon={<Icon icon={SmileIcon} name="smile" />} aria-haspopup="dialog">{column.icon ? "Change icon" : "Add icon"}</DropdownMenuSubTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuSubContent role="dialog" aria-label={`${column.label} icon`}>
            <IconPicker data-column-action="icon" value={column.icon ?? ""} icons={COLUMN_ICONS} />
          </DropdownMenuSubContent>
        </DropdownMenuSub>
        <DropdownMenuSeparator />
        <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!frame} data-column-action="filter"><Icon icon={FilterHorizontalIcon} name="settings-2" />Filter</DropdownMenuItem>
        <DropdownMenuSub>
          <DropdownMenuSubTrigger icon={<Icon icon={SortByDown02Icon} name="sort" />}>Sort</DropdownMenuSubTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuSubContent>
            <DropdownMenuItem role="menuitemradio" closeOnSelect selected={sort?.key === column.key && sort.direction === "ascending"} data-column-action="sort-ascending"><Icon icon={ArrangeByLettersAZIcon} name="sort-a-z" />Sort ascending</DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem role="menuitemradio" closeOnSelect selected={sort?.key === column.key && sort.direction === "descending"} data-column-action="sort-descending"><Icon icon={ArrangeByLettersZAIcon} name="sort-z-a" />Sort descending</DropdownMenuItem>
          </DropdownMenuSubContent>
        </DropdownMenuSub>
        <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!frame} data-column-action="group"><Icon icon={GroupItemsIcon} name="group" />Group</DropdownMenuItem>
        <DropdownMenuCheckboxItem checked={isPinned(column.key)} data-column-action="freeze"><Icon icon={PinIcon} name="pin" />Freeze</DropdownMenuCheckboxItem>
        <DropdownMenuCheckboxItem checked={wrapped.includes(column.key)} data-column-action="wrap"><Icon icon={TextWrapIcon} name="text-wrap" />Wrap content</DropdownMenuCheckboxItem>
        <DropdownMenuSeparator />
        <DropdownMenuItem data-column-action="insert-left"><Icon icon={InsertColumnLeftIcon} name="column-insert-left" directional />Insert left</DropdownMenuItem>
        <DropdownMenuItem data-column-action="insert-right"><Icon icon={InsertColumnRightIcon} name="column-insert-right" directional />Insert right</DropdownMenuItem>
        <DropdownMenuSeparator />
        <DropdownMenuItem data-column-action="duplicate"><Icon icon={CopyPlusIcon} name="copy-plus" />Duplicate</DropdownMenuItem>
        <DropdownMenuItem data-column-action="hide"><Icon icon={ViewOffIcon} name="eye-closed" />Hide</DropdownMenuItem>
        <DropdownMenuItem destructive data-column-action="delete"><Icon icon={ColumnDeleteIcon} name="column-delete" />Delete</DropdownMenuItem>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  );

  const onColumnReorder = ({ from, to }: { from: number; to: number }) => {
    const offset = (rowReorder ? 1 : 0) + (expandable ? 1 : 0) + (selectable ? 1 : 0);
    const fromCol = from - offset;
    const toCol = to - offset;
    if (fromCol >= 0 && toCol >= 0 && fromCol < visibleKeys.length && toCol < visibleKeys.length) {
      setColumnOrder((prev) => {
        const next = [...prev];
        const fromKey = visibleKeys[fromCol];
        const toKey = visibleKeys[toCol];
        const fi = next.indexOf(fromKey);
        const ti = next.indexOf(toKey);
        if (fi >= 0 && ti >= 0) {
          const [moved] = next.splice(fi, 1);
          next.splice(ti, 0, moved);
        }
        return next;
      });
    }
    return false;
  };
  const matches = accounts.filter((a) => {
    const matchesQuery = `${a.name} ${a.email} ${a.id}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase());
    const matchesStatus = statusFilter.length === 0 || statusFilter.includes(a.status);
    const matchesPlan = planFilter.length === 0 || planFilter.includes(a.plan);
    return matchesQuery && matchesStatus && matchesPlan;
  });
  const filterCount = statusFilter.length + planFilter.length;
  const sorted = sort ? [...matches].sort((a, b) => compareValues(valueOf(a, sort.key), valueOf(b, sort.key), sort.direction)) : matches;
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(sorted.length / pageSize));
  const current = Math.min(page, pages);
  const shown = frame ? sorted.slice((current - 1) * pageSize, current * pageSize) : sorted;
  const groupLabel = (account: Account) => {
    const value = valueOf(account, groupKey);
    return value === "" || value == null ? "Empty" : String(value);
  };
  const groupLabels = groupKey === "status" ? GROUP_ORDER : [...new Set(shown.map(groupLabel))];
  const groups = grouped
    ? groupLabels.map((status) => ({ status, rows: shown.filter((a) => groupLabel(a) === status) })).filter((g) => g.rows.length)
    : [{ status: null, rows: shown }];
  const columnCount = visible.length + 2 + (rowReorder ? 1 : 0) + (selectable ? 1 : 0) + (expandable ? 1 : 0);
  const selectableIds = shown.filter((a) => !(disabledRows && a.status === "Closed")).map((a) => a.id);
  const allOn = selectableIds.length > 0 && selectableIds.every((id) => selected.includes(id));
  const someOn = selectableIds.some((id) => selected.includes(id));

  const isClosed = (account: Account) => disabledRows && account.status === "Closed";
  // Dropping a row writes the order you see into the list and turns sorting off, so the new order sticks. Closed rows can't be picked up and keep their slot.
  const onRowReorder = ({ from, to }: TableRowReorderDetail) => {
    const onScreen = groups.flatMap((g) => g.rows);
    const ids = onScreen.filter((a) => !isClosed(a)).map((a) => a.id);
    const [moved] = ids.splice(from, 1);
    ids.splice(to, 0, moved);
    const order = onScreen.map((a) => (isClosed(a) ? a.id : ids.shift()!));
    const byId = new Map(accounts.map((a) => [a.id, a]));
    const start = frame ? (current - 1) * pageSize : 0;
    const full = sorted.map((a) => a.id);
    full.splice(start, order.length, ...order);
    setAccounts([...full.map((id) => byId.get(id)!), ...accounts.filter((a) => !full.includes(a.id))]);
    setSort(null);
    return false;
  };
  const flip = (list: string[], id: string, on: boolean) => (on ? [...list, id] : list.filter((x) => x !== id));
  const cell = (account: Account, key: ColumnKey, closed: boolean) => {
    switch (key) {
      case "name":
        return (
          <TableCell key={key} {...columnProps(key)} pin={pinName}>
            <TableMedia>
              <Avatar name={account.name} size="sm" />
              <div>
                {rowLinks && !pinName && !closed ? (
                  <TableRowLink href={`#${account.id.toLowerCase()}`}>{account.name}</TableRowLink>
                ) : (
                  <span>{account.name}</span>
                )}
                <TableSecondary>{account.email}</TableSecondary>
              </div>
            </TableMedia>
          </TableCell>
        );
      case "plan":
        return (
          <TableCell key={key} {...columnProps(key)}>
            {editable && !closed ? (
              <TableEdit
                multiline={wrapped.includes(key)}
                aria-label={`Plan for ${account.name}`}
                value={account.plan}
                onValueCommit={(v) => updateAccount(account.id, { plan: v || account.plan })}
              />
            ) : (
              account.plan
            )}
          </TableCell>
        );
      case "status":
        return (
          <TableCell key={key} {...columnProps(key)}>
            <Badge variant={STATUS_BADGE[account.status]}>{account.status}</Badge>
          </TableCell>
        );
      case "seats":
        return (
          <TableCell key={key} {...columnProps(key)} numeric data-sort-value={account.seats}>
            {editable && !closed ? (
              <TableEdit
                multiline={wrapped.includes(key)}
                aria-label={`Seats for ${account.name}`}
                inputMode="numeric"
                value={String(account.seats)}
                onValueCommit={(v) => {
                  const n = Number(v.replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, ""));
                  if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0) updateAccount(account.id, { seats: Math.round(n) });
                }}
              />
            ) : (
              account.seats
            )}
          </TableCell>
        );
      case "mrr":
        return (
          <TableCell key={key} {...columnProps(key)} numeric data-sort-value={account.mrr}>
            {editable && !closed ? (
              <TableEdit
                multiline={wrapped.includes(key)}
                aria-label={`MRR for ${account.name}`}
                value={money.format(account.mrr)}
                onValueCommit={(v) => {
                  const n = Number(v.replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, ""));
                  if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0) updateAccount(account.id, { mrr: Math.round(n) });
                }}
              />
            ) : (
              money.format(account.mrr)
            )}
          </TableCell>
        );
      case "renews":
        return (
          <TableCell key={key} {...columnProps(key)} className="ayy-muted">
            {account.renews === "—" ? "—" : day.format(new Date(`${account.renews}T00:00:00`))}
          </TableCell>
        );
      case "note":
        return (
          <TableCell key={key} {...columnProps(key)} className="ayy-muted" truncate={!editable && truncate}>
            {editable && !closed ? (
              <TableEdit
                multiline={wrapped.includes(key)}
                aria-label={`Last note for ${account.name}`}
                value={account.note}
                onValueCommit={(v) => updateAccount(account.id, { note: v })}
              />
            ) : (
              account.note
            )}
          </TableCell>
        );
      default:
        return <TableCell key={key} {...columnProps(key)}><TableEdit multiline={wrapped.includes(key)} aria-label={`${columns.find((c) => c.key === key)?.label} for ${account.name}`} value={values[account.id]?.[key] ?? ""} disabled={closed} onValueCommit={(value) => setValues((all) => ({ ...all, [account.id]: { ...all[account.id], [key]: value } }))} /></TableCell>;
    }
  };

  const row = (account: Account) => {
    const closed = disabledRows && account.status === "Closed";
    const open = expanded.includes(account.id);
    return (
      <Fragment key={account.id}>
        <TableRow data-id={account.id} selected={selectable ? selected.includes(account.id) : undefined} disabled={closed}>
          {rowReorder ? (
            <TableCell reorder>
              <DragHandle aria-label={`Move ${account.name}`} disabled={closed} tooltip={false} />
            </TableCell>
          ) : null}
          {expandable ? (
            <TableCell>
              <TableExpand
                expanded={open}
                aria-controls={`${account.id}-detail`}
                aria-label={`Show details for ${account.name}`}
                onClick={() => setExpanded((list) => flip(list, account.id, !open))}
              />
            </TableCell>
          ) : null}
          {selectable ? (
            <TableCell select>
              <Checkbox
                aria-label={`Select ${account.name}`}
                checked={selected.includes(account.id)}
                disabled={closed}
                onCheckedChange={(on) => setSelected((list) => flip(list, account.id, on))}
              />
            </TableCell>
          ) : null}
          {visible.map((c) => cell(account, c.key, closed))}
          <TableCell className="ayy-table__add-column" />
          <TableCell actions pin={pinActions ? "end" : undefined}>
            <TableRowActions reveal={reveal}>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" aria-label={`Edit ${account.name}`} disabled={closed} onClick={() => toast(`Edit ${account.name}`)}>
                <Icon icon={PencilEdit02Icon} name="square-pen" />
              </Button>
              <DropdownMenu>
                <DropdownMenuTrigger variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" aria-label={`More actions for ${account.name}`} disabled={closed}>
                  <Icon icon={MoreHorizontalIcon} name="ellipsis" />
                </DropdownMenuTrigger>
                <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
                  <DropdownMenuItem onSelect={() => toast.success(`Duplicated ${account.name}`)}>
                    <Icon icon={Copy01Icon} name="copy" />
                    Duplicate
                  </DropdownMenuItem>
                  <DropdownMenuItem onSelect={() => toast(`Archived ${account.name}`)}>
                    <Icon icon={Archive02Icon} name="archive" />
                    Archive
                  </DropdownMenuItem>
                  <DropdownMenuSeparator />
                  <DropdownMenuItem destructive onSelect={() => toast.error(`Deleted ${account.name}`)}>
                    <Icon icon={Delete02Icon} name="trash" />
                    Delete
                  </DropdownMenuItem>
                </DropdownMenuContent>
              </DropdownMenu>
            </TableRowActions>
          </TableCell>
        </TableRow>
        {expandable ? (
          <TableRow detail id={`${account.id}-detail`}>
            <TableCell />
            {selectable ? <TableCell /> : null}
            <TableCell colSpan={visible.length + 2}>
              <span className="ayy-small">
                {account.id} · {account.note}
              </span>
            </TableCell>
          </TableRow>
        ) : null}
      </Fragment>
    );
  };

  const table = (
    <Table
      key={`${resizable}-${reorderable}-${selectable}-${expandable}-${hidden.join()}`}
      compact={density === "compact"}
      spacious={density === "spacious"}
      bordered={bordered}
      sticky={sticky}
      reorderable={reorderable}
      onColumnReorder={onColumnReorder}
      onRowReorder={onRowReorder}
      onColumnAction={onColumnAction}
      aria-busy={loading || undefined}
      aria-labelledby={frame ? "tp-title" : undefined}
      wrapperProps={!frame && sticky ? { style: { maxBlockSize: "20rem" } } : undefined}
    >
      {frame ? null : <TableCaption className="ayy-sr-only">Accounts</TableCaption>}
      <TableHeader>
        <TableRow>
          {rowReorder ? (
            <TableHead reorder reorderable={false}>
              <span className="ayy-sr-only">Reorder</span>
            </TableHead>
          ) : null}
          {expandable ? (
            <TableHead reorderable={false}>
              <span className="ayy-sr-only">Details</span>
            </TableHead>
          ) : null}
          {selectable ? (
            <TableHead select>
              <Checkbox
                aria-label="Select all accounts on this page"
                checked={allOn}
                indeterminate={someOn && !allOn}
                onCheckedChange={(on) => setSelected((list) => (on ? [...new Set([...list, ...selectableIds])] : list.filter((id) => !selectableIds.includes(id))))}
              />
            </TableHead>
          ) : null}
          {visible.map((c) => (
            <TableHead
              key={c.key}
              numeric={c.numeric}
              pin={isPinned(c.key)}
              wrap={wrapped.includes(c.key)}
              aria-sort={sort?.key === c.key ? sort.direction : undefined}
              reorderable={reorderable && !isPinned(c.key)}
              data-key={c.key}
            >
              {columnMenu(c)}
              {resizable ? <TableResize aria-label={`Resize ${c.label}`} /> : null}
            </TableHead>
          ))}
          <TableHead className="ayy-table__add-column">
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" aria-label="Add column" data-column-action="add"><Icon icon={Add01Icon} name="add" /></Button>
          </TableHead>
          <TableHead actions pin={pinActions ? "end" : undefined}>
            <span className="ayy-sr-only">Actions</span>
          </TableHead>
        </TableRow>
      </TableHeader>
      {!visible.length ? (
        <TableBody><TableEmpty colSpan={columnCount}><EmptyState compact><EmptyStateTitle>No columns</EmptyStateTitle><EmptyStateDescription>Use the + in the header to add a column, or show columns from the Columns menu.</EmptyStateDescription></EmptyState></TableEmpty></TableBody>
      ) : shown.length ? (
        groups.map((g) => (
          <TableBody key={g.status ?? "all"}>
            {g.status ? <TableGroup colSpan={columnCount}>{g.status} · {g.rows.length}</TableGroup> : null}
            {g.rows.map(row)}
          </TableBody>
        ))
      ) : (
        <TableBody>
          <TableEmpty colSpan={columnCount}>
            <EmptyState compact>
              <EmptyStateTitle>No accounts match “{query}”</EmptyStateTitle>
              <EmptyStateDescription>Check the spelling, or search by email or account ID.</EmptyStateDescription>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() => {
                  setQuery("");
                  setStatusFilter([]);
                  setPlanFilter([]);
                }}
              >
                Clear search and filters
              </Button>
            </EmptyState>
          </TableEmpty>
        </TableBody>
      )}
      <TableFooter>
        <TableRow>
          {rowReorder ? <TableCell reorder /> : null}
          {expandable ? <TableCell /> : null}
          {selectable ? <TableCell select /> : null}
          {visible.map((c) => {
            if (c.key === "name") {
              return (
                <TableCell key={c.key} {...columnProps(c.key)} pin={pinName}>
                  Total
                </TableCell>
              );
            }
            if (c.key === "seats") {
              return (
                <TableCell key={c.key} {...columnProps(c.key)} numeric data-sort-value={matches.reduce((s, a) => s + a.seats, 0)}>
                  {matches.reduce((s, a) => s + a.seats, 0)}
                </TableCell>
              );
            }
            if (c.key === "mrr") {
              return (
                <TableCell key={c.key} {...columnProps(c.key)} numeric data-sort-value={matches.reduce((s, a) => s + a.mrr, 0)}>
                  {money.format(matches.reduce((s, a) => s + a.mrr, 0))}
                </TableCell>
              );
            }
            return <TableCell key={c.key} {...columnProps(c.key)} />;
          })}
          <TableCell className="ayy-table__add-column" />
          <TableCell actions pin={pinActions ? "end" : undefined} />
        </TableRow>
      </TableFooter>
    </Table>
  );

  const actionBar = (
    <ActionBar open={selectable && selected.length > 0} onClear={() => setSelected([])}>
      <ActionBarSelection>
        <ActionBarCount>{selected.length} selected</ActionBarCount>
        <ActionBarClose />
      </ActionBarSelection>
      <ActionBarSeparator />
      <ActionBarActions>
        <ActionBarButton onClick={() => toast(`Editing ${selected.length} accounts`)}>
          <Icon icon={PencilEdit02Icon} name="square-pen" />
          Edit
        </ActionBarButton>
        <ActionBarButton onClick={() => toast.success(`Exported ${selected.length} accounts`)}>
          <Icon icon={Copy01Icon} name="copy" />
          Export
        </ActionBarButton>
        <ActionBarButton onClick={() => toast(`Archived ${selected.length} accounts`)}>
          <Icon icon={Archive02Icon} name="archive" />
          Archive
        </ActionBarButton>
        <ActionBarButton
          destructive
          onClick={() => {
            toast.error(`Deleted ${selected.length} accounts`);
            setSelected([]);
          }}
        >
          <Icon icon={Delete02Icon} name="trash" />
          Delete
        </ActionBarButton>
      </ActionBarActions>
    </ActionBar>
  );

  if (!frame) {
    return (
      <div style={{ inlineSize: "100%" }}>
        {table}
        {actionBar}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <TableContainer style={{ inlineSize: "100%", maxBlockSize: sticky ? "32rem" : undefined }}>
        <TableToolbar>
          <TableHeading>
            <TableTitle id="tp-title">Accounts</TableTitle>
            <TableDescription>
              {selected.length ? `${selected.length} selected · ` : ""}
              {matches.length} of {ACCOUNTS.length} accounts
              {statusFilter.length > 0 || planFilter.length > 0 ? " (filtered)" : ""}
            </TableDescription>
          </TableHeading>
          <TableTools>
            <div style={{ inlineSize: "14rem" }}>
              <SearchBar
                label="Search accounts"
                placeholder="Search accounts"
                value={query}
                onValueChange={(v) => {
                  setQuery(v);
                  setPage(1);
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <DropdownMenu>
              <DropdownMenuTrigger ref={filterTrigger} variant={filterCount ? "secondary" : "outline"} size="icon" aria-label={filterCount ? `Filters (${filterCount} active)` : "Filters"} data-table-control="filters">
                <Icon icon={FilterHorizontalIcon} name="settings-2" />
              </DropdownMenuTrigger>
              <DropdownMenuContent align="end" aria-label="Filter options" style={{ minInlineSize: "13rem" }}>
                <DropdownMenuLabel>Filter by</DropdownMenuLabel>
                <DropdownMenuSub>
                  <DropdownMenuSubTrigger icon={<Icon icon={Flag01Icon} name="flag" />} value={statusFilter.length || undefined}>Status</DropdownMenuSubTrigger>
                  <DropdownMenuSubContent aria-label="Status options">
                    {(["Active", "Trial", "Past due", "Closed"] as Status[]).map((st) => (
                      <DropdownMenuSelectedItem
                        key={st}
                        selected={statusFilter.includes(st)}
                        onSelectedChange={(on) => {
                          setStatusFilter((list) => (on ? [...list, st] : list.filter((s) => s !== st)));
                          setPage(1);
                        }}
                        value={accounts.filter((a) => a.status === st).length}
                      >
                        {st}
                      </DropdownMenuSelectedItem>
                    ))}
                  </DropdownMenuSubContent>
                </DropdownMenuSub>
                <DropdownMenuSub>
                  <DropdownMenuSubTrigger icon={<Icon icon={Tag01Icon} name="tag" />} value={planFilter.length || undefined}>Plan</DropdownMenuSubTrigger>
                  <DropdownMenuSubContent aria-label="Plan options">
                    {["Enterprise", "Team", "Starter"].map((pl) => (
                      <DropdownMenuSelectedItem
                        key={pl}
                        selected={planFilter.includes(pl)}
                        onSelectedChange={(on) => {
                          setPlanFilter((list) => (on ? [...list, pl] : list.filter((p) => p !== pl)));
                          setPage(1);
                        }}
                        value={accounts.filter((a) => a.plan === pl).length}
                      >
                        {pl}
                      </DropdownMenuSelectedItem>
                    ))}
                  </DropdownMenuSubContent>
                </DropdownMenuSub>
                {filterCount ? (
                  <>
                    <DropdownMenuSeparator />
                    <DropdownMenuItem destructive onSelect={() => { setStatusFilter([]); setPlanFilter([]); setPage(1); }}>Reset filters</DropdownMenuItem>
                  </>
                ) : null}
              </DropdownMenuContent>
            </DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenu>
              <DropdownMenuTrigger variant={sort ? "secondary" : "outline"} size="icon" aria-label={sort ? `Sort (${columns.find((c) => c.key === sort.key)?.label ?? sort.key}, ${sort.direction})` : "Sort"} data-table-control="sort">
                <Icon icon={ArrowUpDownIcon} name="arrow-up-down" />
              </DropdownMenuTrigger>
              <DropdownMenuContent align="end" aria-label="Sort options" style={{ minInlineSize: "13rem" }}>
                <DropdownMenuLabel>Sort by</DropdownMenuLabel>
                {columns.map((c) => (
                  <DropdownMenuSub key={c.key}>
                    <DropdownMenuSubTrigger>{c.label}</DropdownMenuSubTrigger>
                    <DropdownMenuSubContent aria-label={`Sort ${c.label}`}>
                      <DropdownMenuRadioGroup value={sort?.key === c.key ? sort.direction : ""}>
                        <DropdownMenuRadioItem value="ascending" data-column-action="sort-ascending" data-column-key={c.key}>{c.numeric ? "Low to high" : "A to Z"}</DropdownMenuRadioItem>
                        <DropdownMenuRadioItem value="descending" data-column-action="sort-descending" data-column-key={c.key}>{c.numeric ? "High to low" : "Z to A"}</DropdownMenuRadioItem>
                      </DropdownMenuRadioGroup>
                    </DropdownMenuSubContent>
                  </DropdownMenuSub>
                ))}
              </DropdownMenuContent>
            </DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenu>
              <DropdownMenuTrigger ref={groupTrigger} variant="outline" size="icon" aria-label="Table options" data-table-control="groups">
                <Icon icon={LayoutTable01Icon} name="layout-table" />
              </DropdownMenuTrigger>
              <DropdownMenuContent align="end" aria-label="Table options" style={{ minInlineSize: "13rem" }}>
                <DropdownMenuSub>
                  <DropdownMenuSubTrigger icon={<Icon icon={GroupItemsIcon} name="group" />}>Group by</DropdownMenuSubTrigger>
                  <DropdownMenuSubContent aria-label="Group by">
                    <DropdownMenuRadioGroup value={grouped ? groupKey : "none"}>
                      <DropdownMenuRadioItem value="none" data-column-action="group-by" data-column-value="none">No groups</DropdownMenuRadioItem>
                      {columns.map((c) => <DropdownMenuRadioItem key={c.key} value={c.key} data-column-action="group-by" data-column-value={c.key}>{c.label}</DropdownMenuRadioItem>)}
                    </DropdownMenuRadioGroup>
                  </DropdownMenuSubContent>
                </DropdownMenuSub>
                <DropdownMenuSub>
                  <DropdownMenuSubTrigger icon={<Icon icon={LayoutTable01Icon} name="layout-table" />}>Columns</DropdownMenuSubTrigger>
                  <DropdownMenuSubContent aria-label="Columns">
                    {columns.map((c) => (
                      <DropdownMenuCheckboxItem
                        key={c.key}
                        checked={!hidden.includes(c.key)}
                        onCheckedChange={(on) => setHidden((list) => (on ? list.filter((k) => k !== c.key) : [...list, c.key]))}
                      >
                        {c.label}
                      </DropdownMenuCheckboxItem>
                    ))}
                  </DropdownMenuSubContent>
                </DropdownMenuSub>
              </DropdownMenuContent>
            </DropdownMenu>
            <Button onClick={() => toast("New account")}>
              <Icon icon={Add01Icon} name="add" />
              Add account
            </Button>
          </TableTools>
        </TableToolbar>
        {table}
        <TablePager>
          <span>
            {sorted.length ? `${(current - 1) * pageSize + 1}–${Math.min(current * pageSize, sorted.length)} of ${sorted.length}` : "0 of 0"}
          </span>
          <Pagination
            page={current}
            count={pages}
            onPageChange={setPage}
            pageSize={pageSize}
            pageSizeOptions={PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS}
            onPageSizeChange={(size) => {
              setPageSize(size);
              setPage(1);
            }}
          />
        </TablePager>
      </TableContainer>
      {actionBar}
    </>
  );
}
```

---
Part of @danitesler/ayywi 0.1.2: load `dist/ayywi.min.css` (and `dist/elements.global.js` for the <ayy-*> elements). The rules every screen follows: [llms-full.txt#rules](../llms-full.txt#rules). Every component: [llms.txt](../llms.txt).
